    60	ACTION_FILE_TICKET = "file_ticket"
    61	ACTION_RELABEL = "relabel"
    62	ACTION_TRANSITION = "transition"
    63	ACTION_MENTION_OPERATOR = "mention_operator"
    64	ACTION_MARK_FOR_FOLLOWUP = "mark_for_followup"
    65	ACTION_ESCALATE = "escalate"
    66	
    67	ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    68	    {
    69	        ACTION_NOOP,
    70	        ACTION_FILE_TICKET,
    71	        ACTION_RELABEL,
    72	        ACTION_TRANSITION,
    73	        ACTION_MENTION_OPERATOR,
    74	        ACTION_MARK_FOR_FOLLOWUP,
    75	        ACTION_ESCALATE,
    76	    }
    77	)
    78	
    79	
    80	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    81	class Action:
    82	    """A single thing the coordinator decides to do.
    83	
    84	    The skeleton only ever emits :class:`NoopAction`; the Tier-1 rule set
    85	    (29f-3) emits the §6.1 action kinds via the factory classmethods below.
    86	    The envelope is unchanged: ``kind`` is one of :data:`ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS`,
    87	    ``target`` is the JIRA key the action applies to, ``params`` carries the
    88	    kind-specific payload, and ``dry_run`` defaults to True so a half-wired
    89	    (or shadow-mode, 29f-14) daemon can never mutate JIRA/Gerrit.
    90	
    91	    "Union type" (Code AC): :class:`Action` + :class:`NoopAction` over the
    92	    constrained :data:`ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS` *is* the action union — a closed
    93	    set discriminated by ``kind`` rather than a ``typing.Union`` of N classes,
    94	    so the decision-log serialisation (:meth:`to_record`) stays uniform.
    95	    """
    96	
    97	    kind: str
    98	    target: str = ""
    99	    params: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
   100	    dry_run: bool = True
   101	
   102	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   103	        if not isinstance(self.kind, str) or not self.kind.strip():
   104	            raise ValueError("Action.kind must be a non-empty string")
   105	        if self.kind not in ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS:
   106	            raise ValueError(
   107	                f"Action.kind {self.kind!r} is not in the §6.1 allowed set "
   108	                f"{sorted(ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS)}"
   109	            )
   110	        object.__setattr__(self, "params", dict(self.params))
   111	
   112	    def to_record(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   113	        """Serialisable form embedded in a decision-log ``actions`` array."""
   114	        return {
   115	            "kind": self.kind,
   116	            "target": self.target,
   117	            "params": dict(self.params),
   118	            "dry_run": self.dry_run,
   119	        }
   120	
   250	    # The wider work-graph slice the cross-ticket rules traverse
   251	    # (runner-blocked-marker-stale resolves the blocker's status;
   252	    # chain-deadlock walks the blockedBy DAG). Keyed by JIRA key.
   253	    tickets: Mapping[str, "Ticket"] = field(default_factory=dict)
   254	
   255	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   256	        if self.now.tzinfo is None:
   257	            raise ValueError("DecisionContext.now must be timezone-aware")
   258	        object.__setattr__(self, "work_graph", dict(self.work_graph))
   259	        object.__setattr__(self, "tickets", dict(self.tickets))

   262	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   263	class DecisionResult:
   264	    """Outcome of one ``evaluate`` call.
   265	
   266	    ``decision_id`` is a fresh UUID per tick so a decision-log line can be
   267	    correlated with downstream action records (and L6 crash-recovery can
   268	    pair ``shutdown_began`` / ``shutdown_complete`` with the right tick).
   269	    """
   270	
   271	    decision_id: str
   272	    actions: tuple[Action, ...]
   273	    reason: str
   274	    mode: str
   275	    engine_version: str
   595	# The veto/halt/safety rules sit above the routing rules so a coord-skip or a
   596	# deadlock is never overridden by a lower-stakes routing action.
   597	# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

   599	
   600	@rule(name="operator-keep-out", priority=0)
   601	def operator_keep_out(ctx: DecisionContext) -> "Action | None":
   602	    """§5.1 #10 — ``coord-skip`` present: take no action, ever.
   603	
   604	    Returns a :class:`NoopAction` (not ``None``) so, sitting at priority 0,
   605	    it short-circuits the whole registry: an operator escape-hatched ticket
   606	    can never be touched by a lower-priority rule (ADR §6.2 self-modify ban,
   607	    §6.3 operator override). Abstains (``None``) when there's no focal ticket
   608	    or the label is absent, so other rules still run.
   609	    """
   610	    t = ctx.ticket
   611	    if t is None or not has_label(t, COORD_SKIP_LABEL):
   612	        return None
   613	    return NoopAction()
   614	
   615	
   616	@rule(name="chain-deadlock", priority=5)
   617	def chain_deadlock(ctx: DecisionContext) -> "Action | None":
   618	    """§5.1 #8 — A blocks B blocks A (or longer cycle): diagnose + escalate.
   619	
   620	    Walks the blockedBy DAG from the focal ticket over ``ctx.tickets``. If
   621	    the focal key is reachable from itself, that's a cycle — file a
   622	    diagnostic + @-mention operator (this should never happen; if it does,
   623	    halt rather than guess). Abstains when the graph is acyclic / unknown.
   624	    """
   625	    t = ctx.ticket
   880	
   881	    def __init__(self, registry: tuple[RegisteredRule, ...] | None = None) -> None:
   882	        self._rules = registry if registry is not None else load_registry()
   883	        logger.info(
   884	            "[pipeline_coordinator] Tier-1 rule registry loaded: %d rules "
   885	            "(engine=%s) [%s]",
   886	            len(self._rules),
   887	            self.engine_version,
   888	            ", ".join(f"{r.name}@{r.priority}" for r in self._rules),
   889	        )
   890	
   891	    @property
   892	    def rules(self) -> tuple[RegisteredRule, ...]:
   893	        return self._rules
   894	
   895	    def rule_names(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   896	        return tuple(r.name for r in self._rules)
   897	
   898	    def evaluate(self, ctx: DecisionContext) -> DecisionResult:
   899	        """Iterate the registry in priority order; first Action wins."""
   900	        for r in self._rules:
   901	            action = r.evaluate(ctx)
   902	            if action is None:
   903	                continue
   904	            return DecisionResult(
   905	                decision_id=uuid.uuid4().hex,
   906	                actions=(action,),
   907	                reason=f"tier1:{r.name}",
   908	                mode=ctx.mode,
   909	                engine_version=self.engine_version,
   910	                rule_name=r.name,
   911	                tier=1,
   912	            )
   913	        # No Tier-1 rule matched → Tier-2 LLM consult territory (29f-6).
   914	        return DecisionResult(
   915	            decision_id=uuid.uuid4().hex,

   970	
   971	def _last_json_line(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   972	    try:
   973	        lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
   974	    except OSError:
   975	        return None
   976	    for line in reversed(lines):
   977	        if line.strip():
   978	            try:
   979	                rec = json.loads(line)
   980	            except json.JSONDecodeError:
   981	                return None
   982	            return rec if isinstance(rec, dict) else None
   983	    return None

   986	def _ticket_has_live_runner(key: str, *, repo_root: Path | None = None) -> bool:
   987	    """ADR §3.3 Startup-2: a runner is live for ``key`` if a process cmdline
   988	    references it (auto-runner / its fresh feature branch) or an ephemeral
   989	    worktree sentinel for the ticket exists. Best-effort; any probe error
   990	    means "unknown" → treated as *not* live so reconciliation can proceed."""
   991	    branch = f"feature/{key}-runner-fresh"
   992	    proc_dir = Path("/proc")
   993	    if proc_dir.is_dir():
   994	        for entry in proc_dir.iterdir():
   995	            if not entry.name.isdigit():
   996	                continue
   997	            try:
   998	                cmdline = (entry / "cmdline").read_bytes().replace(b"\0", b" ").decode(
   999	                    "utf-8", "replace"
  1000	                )
  1001	            except OSError:
  1002	                continue
  1003	            if key in cmdline and ("auto-runner" in cmdline or branch in cmdline):
  1004	                return True
  1005	    # Worktree sentinel: an ephemeral worktree dir named for the ticket.
  1006	    try:
  1007	        from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
  1008	
  1009	        base = jira_dispatch.EPHEMERAL_WORKTREE_BASE
  1010	        if base.is_dir():
  1011	            for child in base.iterdir():
  1012	                if child.is_dir() and key in child.name:
  1013	                    return True
  1014	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
  1015	        pass
  1016	    return False

  1025	            ["git", "-C", str(repo_root), "merge-base", branch, "develop"],
  1026	            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15,
  1027	        )
  1028	        if base.returncode != 0:
  1029	            return False
  1030	        merge_base = base.stdout.strip()

  1349	    # ── decision-log record (ADR-0021 §3.2 example) ──
  1350	
  1351	    def _build_tick_record(
  1352	        self,
  1353	        result: DecisionResult,
  1354	        action_results: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
  1355	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1356	        # A no-op tick records ``actions:[]``; only real (non-noop) actions
  1357	        # are serialised into the array (skeleton emits none).
  1358	        actions = [a.to_record() for a in result.actions if not isinstance(a, NoopAction)]
  1359	        dry_run = all(a.dry_run for a in result.actions) if result.actions else True
  1360	        record: dict[str, Any] = {
  1361	            "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  1362	            "event": "decision_tick",
  1363	            "engine_version": result.engine_version,
  1364	            "mode": result.mode,
  1365	            "decision_id": result.decision_id,
  1366	            "actions": actions,
  1367	            "reason": result.reason,
  1368	            "dry_run": dry_run,
  1369	            "pid": os.getpid(),
  1370	        }
  1371	        # When a personality mode fired (non-idle tick), record the behavior
  1372	        # knobs that steered Tier-1 / Tier-2 so the selected mode is auditable
  1373	        # in the shadow/decision log (ADR-0021 §7.2 + Integration AC).
  1374	        if result.mode_behavior is not None:
  1375	            record["mode_behavior"] = {
  1376	                "tier1_dominant": result.mode_behavior.tier1_dominant,
  1377	                "tier2_policy": result.mode_behavior.tier2_policy,
  1378	                "llm_context_hops": result.mode_behavior.llm_context_hops,
  1379	                "llm_lessons_window": result.mode_behavior.llm_lessons_window,
  1380	            }
  1381	        # 29f-3: name the firing rule + tier (ADR Appendix C) and the action
  1382	        # layer's per-action outcomes — only when a rule actually fired, so a
  1383	        # plain no-op tick keeps the skeleton's minimal record shape.
  1384	        if result.rule_name is not None:
  1385	            record["rule_name"] = result.rule_name
  1386	        if result.tier is not None:
  1387	            record["tier"] = result.tier
  1388	        # 29f-6: the Tier-2 LLM-consultation block (ADR Appendix C) — only on a
  1389	        # tick that actually consulted (or degraded) the LLM, so a Tier-1 / no-op
  1390	        # tick keeps its minimal record shape and ``budget_cap`` events stay
  1391	        # grep-able + replayable for the daily-spend rebuild.
  1392	        if result.llm_consultation is not None:
  1393	            record["llm_consultation"] = dict(result.llm_consultation)
  1394	        if action_results:
  1395	            record["action_results"] = action_results
  1396	        return record
  1397	
  1398	    # ── cold-start 4-phase recovery (ADR §3.3 + §9 L6) ──
  1399	
  1400	    def _log_cold_start(self, record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
  1401	        """Append a cold-start decision-log record, stamping ts/pid/engine."""
  1402	        base: dict[str, Any] = {
  1403	            "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  1404	            "engine_version": self._engine.engine_version,
  1405	            "pid": os.getpid(),
  1406	        }
  1407	        base.update(record)
  1408	        self._decision_log.append(base)
  1409	
  1410	    def startup(self) -> ColdStartReport:
  1411	        """Run the 4-phase cold-start recovery (ADR §3.3). Returns a report.
  1412	
  1413	        Order: Startup-1 (infra) → L6 crash-recovery (ADR §9 L6: "after Phase
  1414	        Startup-1") → Startup-2 (JIRA reconcile) → Startup-3 (stale sweep) →
  1415	        Startup-4 (log steady-state entry). If Startup-1 cannot bring the
  1416	        expected infra up, the daemon halts startup (``phase1_halted=True``)
  1417	        and does NOT proceed — entering the loop with degraded infra is worse
  1418	        than not running (ADR §3.3 Startup-1 failure path). :meth:`run` reads
  1419	        the report to decide whether to enter the loop.
  1420	        """
  1421	        report = ColdStartReport()
  1422	        self._log_cold_start({"event": COLD_START_BEGAN_EVENT})
  1423	        self._startup_1_infra(report)
  1424	        if report.phase1_halted:
  1425	            self._log_cold_start(
  1470	            self._log_cold_start(
  1471	                {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 1, "action": "halt",
  1472	                 "still_down": report.still_down_units}
  1473	            )
  1474	
  1475	    def _l6_crash_recovery(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  1476	        """L6 (ADR §9): replay last 24h decision log; resume/complete/rollback.
  1477	
  1478	        A ``shutdown_began`` with no matching ``shutdown_complete`` (paired by
  1479	        ``decision_id``) means the daemon crashed mid-drain. For each such
  1480	        crash we look at the immediately-preceding tick: if it executed a real
  1481	        (non-dry-run) action that was never confirmed complete, we resolve it
  1482	        per action kind — idempotent relabel/transition are *completed*
  1483	        (re-issued safely), file_ticket is effectively irreversible so we
  1484	        *escalate*, everything else is a logged no-op. Every resolution writes
  1485	        a ``crash_recovery_applied`` record (ADR §9 L6).
  1486	        """
  1487	        records = self._read_recent_decision_log()
  1488	        began: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
  1489	        completed: set[str] = set()
  1490	        last_real_action_tick: dict[str, Any] | None = None
  1491	        for rec in records:
  1492	            event = rec.get("event")
  1493	            if event == "decision_tick" and not rec.get("dry_run", True) and rec.get("actions"):
  1494	                last_real_action_tick = rec
  1495	            elif event == "shutdown_began":
  1496	                began[str(rec.get("decision_id", ""))] = rec
  1497	                # Snapshot which real-action tick preceded this drain.
  1498	                rec["_preceding_real_tick"] = last_real_action_tick
  1499	            elif event == "shutdown_complete":
  1500	                completed.add(str(rec.get("decision_id", "")))
  1501	        for drain_id, drain_rec in began.items():
  1502	            if drain_id in completed:
  1503	                continue  # clean shutdown — nothing to recover
  1504	            tick = drain_rec.get("_preceding_real_tick")
  1505	            resolutions = self._resolve_interrupted_actions(tick)
  1506	            entry = {
  1507	                "event": CRASH_RECOVERY_EVENT,
  1508	                "crashed_drain_id": drain_id,
  1509	                "resolutions": resolutions,
  1510	            }
  1511	            report.crash_recoveries.append(entry)
  1512	            self._log_cold_start(entry)
  1513	
  1514	    def _resolve_interrupted_actions(self, tick: dict[str, Any] | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1515	        """Map each interrupted action of a crashed tick to a recovery step."""
  1516	        if not tick:
  1517	            return [{"resolution": "noop", "reason": "no in-flight real action before crash"}]
  1518	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  1519	        out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1520	        for action in tick.get("actions", []):
  1521	            kind = action.get("kind")
  1522	            target = action.get("target", "")
  1523	            if kind in (ACTION_RELABEL, ACTION_TRANSITION):
  1524	                # Idempotent → safe to complete (re-issue) on recovery.
  1525	                if kind == ACTION_TRANSITION and action.get("params", {}).get(
  1526	                    "to_status"
  1527	                ) == "Under Review":
  1528	                    gw.transition_under_review(target)
  1529	                resolution = "completed"
  1530	            elif kind == ACTION_FILE_TICKET:
  1531	                # Not safely reversible/repeatable — surface to operator.
  1532	                gw.mention_operator(
  1533	                    target,
  1534	                    f"[cold-start L6] crash interrupted a file_ticket action on {target}; "
  1535	                    "verify the dependency ticket exists and was not double-filed.",
  1536	                    urgency="medium",
  1537	                )
  1538	                resolution = "escalated"
  1539	            else:
  1540	                resolution = "noop"
  1541	            out.append({"kind": kind, "target": target, "resolution": resolution})
  1542	        return out
  1543	
  1544	    def _read_recent_decision_log(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1545	        """Return decision-log records from the last :data:`L6_REPLAY_WINDOW_HOURS`.
  1670	        Runs :meth:`startup` first (ADR §3.3 — the coordinator is the canonical
  1671	        startup orchestrator). If Startup-1 halted on degraded infra, the loop
  1672	        is NOT entered and 0 ticks are returned. Otherwise hands off to
  1673	        :meth:`run_forever` (Startup-4).
  1674	        """
  1675	        report = self.startup()
  1676	        if report.phase1_halted:
  1677	            logger.error(
  1678	                "[pipeline_coordinator] cold-start halted in Startup-1 "
  1679	                "(infra still down: %s) — not entering steady-state loop",
  1680	                report.still_down_units,
  1681	            )
  1682	            return 0
  1683	        return self.run_forever(install_signals=install_signals, max_ticks=max_ticks)
  1684	
  1685	    # ── entrypoints ──
  1686	
  1687	    def run_once(self) -> DecisionResult:
  1688	        """One tick: heartbeat → refresh world → evaluate → log decision.
  1689	
  1690	        Returns the engine's :class:`DecisionResult` (a no-op in the
  1691	        skeleton) so callers/tests can assert on it directly.
  1692	        """
  1693	        self._heartbeat.touch()
  1694	        self._refresh_work_graph()
  1695	        self._append_source_event_records()
  1696	        ctx = self.build_context()
  1697	        if self._config.capacity_path is not None:
  1698	            write_capacity_snapshot(ctx.capacity, self._config.capacity_path)
  1699	        result = self._engine.evaluate(ctx)
  1700	        # Hand each real (non-noop) action to the action layer. In shadow
  1701	        # mode it only records the would-be execution; the outcomes go into
  1702	        # the decision-log line so a fired rule is observable end-to-end.
  1703	        action_results = [
  1704	            self._action_executor(a, ctx)
  1705	            for a in result.actions
  1706	            if not isinstance(a, NoopAction)
  1707	        ]
  1708	        record = self._build_tick_record(result, action_results)
  1709	        self._decision_log.append(record)
  1710	        self._run_learning_loop(record, result)
  1711	        return result
  1712	
  1713	    def _run_learning_loop(self, record: dict[str, Any], result: DecisionResult) -> None:
  1714	        """Write back a Tier-2 decision + fire any due daily/weekly handler.
  1715	
  1716	        Gated on a wired learning loop (29f-11) so the skeleton / test daemon is
  1717	        unaffected. Write-back happens in-tick so a Tier-2 decision reaches
  1718	        Cognee well within the 60s Integration-AC budget; the daily + weekly
  1719	        schedules are gated by the loop's own cadence (mirrors the hourly
  1720	        sweep). Fail-open: a learning-loop error never breaks a decision tick.
  1721	        """
  1722	        loop = self._learning_loop
  1723	        if loop is None:
  1724	            return
  1725	        try:
  1726	            if not self._learning_seeded:
  1727	                loop.seed_schedule(self._clock())
  1728	                self._learning_seeded = True
  1729	            if result.tier == 2:
  1730	                loop.write_back_decision(record)
  1731	            loop.maybe_run(self._clock())
  1732	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — learning loop is best-effort
  1733	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] learning loop error: %s", exc)
  1734

  1743	                    "ticket_key": event.get("ticket_key"),
  1744	                    "payload": event.get("payload"),
  1745	                    "dry_run": True,

    24	| **B6 / Q14 cold-start caps** | PARTIAL — defaults? | **first-flip defaults `INFRA=1`, `RECONCILE=1`, `SWEEP=1`** (`OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_COLD_START_MAX_{INFRA,RECONCILE,SWEEP}`); raise only after canary evidence. Per-phase counters: Startup-1 `start_unit` (:653), Startup-2 transitions/labels (:735), Startup-3 sweep (:776). |
    25	| **B4 / Q13 fairness cursor** | PARTIAL — persistence? | **cursor state is decision-log-derived, NOT a new file.** ADR-0021 §3.2 (ADR-0021:105) blesses only decision-log/state artifacts for stateless-across-restarts; the round-robin position is rebuilt from the recent decision-log tail (which targets were acted on last), same pattern as the budget guard rebuilding spend from the log. |
    26	| **S10 / Q11 crash correlation** | PARTIAL — correlation undefined | **see §Crash recovery below** — explicit `tick_intent`↔outcome correlation key + why replay is idempotent. |
    27	
    28	---
    29	
    30	## §Crash recovery — correlation + idempotent replay (S10 / Q11 pinned)
    31	
    32	The round-3 ask: define how a `tick_intent` matches its outcome, and how
    33	completed subcalls avoid replay. v4:
    34	
    35	1. **Correlation key** = the tick's `decision_id` (rules.py:266 — fresh per tick,
    36	   so unique per intent). `run_once` writes:
    37	   - `tick_intent` record `{decision_id, planned: [{kind, target, idem_keys:[...]}], ts}` **before** executing (closes the execute-before-append window at :1703-before-:1708);
    38	   - the existing outcome `decision_tick` record `{decision_id, action_results, ...}` **after**, carrying the SAME `decision_id`.
    39	2. **L6 replay rule** (`_resolve_interrupted_actions` :1514; L6 scans
    40	   `decision_tick` + shutdown records today, :1493 — extend it to also scan
    41	   `tick_intent`): a `tick_intent` whose matching `decision_tick` (same
    42	   `decision_id`) is **absent** is a crash window → replay its planned actions.
    43	3. **Why replay is safe (no double-apply)**: replay re-issues under the SAME
    44	   stable idem keys (`coord:{kind}:{target}:{content_hash}:<suffix>`). The
    45	   idempotency store (idempotency.py:90) dedupes any subcall that already
    46	   completed before the crash — so a partially-applied composite action
    47	   completes exactly the missing subcalls. This is the property the v3 B3 fix
    48	   buys us: stable keys make crash-replay correct *by construction*, no
    49	   per-subcall completion bookkeeping needed.
    50	4. `action_results` is persisted in the `decision_tick` record (:1394) for
    51	   observability/diff, but L6 correctness relies on the idem store, not on
    52	   parsing partial `action_results`.
    53	
    54	---
    55	
    56	## §dry_run flip — Option B wiring (Q7 pinned)
    57	
    58	```
    59	result = self._engine.evaluate(ctx)                      # :1699 (unchanged)
    60	actions = result.actions
    61	if self._acting:                                          # new: only in acting mode
    62	    actions = tuple(
    63	        a if isinstance(a, NoopAction) else replace(a, dry_run=False)
    64	        for a in actions
    65	    )
    66	# tick-record / dry_run logging (:1358) now sees the real dry_run=False ─ truthful
    67	action_results = [...]  # cap + per-action try/except (B4/S7), over `actions`
    68	```
    69	- `self._acting` is set from the env-gate (`OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_ACTING`, S8) at
    70	  construction, the single source of truth.
    71	- Executor logic stays uniform: "mutate iff `dry_run=False`". A stray
    72	  `dry_run=True` action in acting mode still can't mutate (it was a NoopAction or
    73	  the replace didn't touch it) — default-deny preserved.
    74	- Decision-log + `tick_intent` now record `dry_run:false` truthfully, which is
    75	  exactly what L6 reads for crash recovery (the reason codex rejected Option A).
    76	
    77	---
    78	
    79	## §coord-skip / destructive recheck — pinned helper names
    80	
    81	Immediately before any mutation, in the executor:
    82	1. `labels = fetch_ticket_labels(client, action.target)` (jira_dispatch.py:1826);
    83	   on exception → `executed:False,reason:"label_read_failed"` (fail-closed).
    84	2. `coord-skip`/`coord-quarantine` ∈ labels → `executed:False,reason:"coord_skip"`.
    85	3. **For `transition → To Do` only** (destructive — clears assignee,
    86	   jira_dispatch.py:1991): additionally require
    87	   `get_issue_status(client, target)` (jira_dispatch.py:1586) ∈ IN_PROGRESS names
    88	   AND `not _ticket_has_live_runner(target)` (pipeline_coordinator.py:986); else
    89	   `executed:False,reason:"context_stale"`.
    90

  1623	def _under_review_idem_key(
  1624	    key: str,
  1625	    *,
  1626	    change_number: int | None = None,
  1627	    change_url: str | None = None,
  1628	) -> str:
  1629	    operation_type = "transition"
  1630	    if change_number is not None:
  1631	        material = json.dumps(
  1632	            {"target_status": "under-review", "patchset": change_number},
  1633	            sort_keys=True,
  1634	            separators=(",", ":"),
  1635	        )
  1636	        args_hash = hashlib.sha256(material.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
  1637	    elif change_url:
  1638	        args_hash = hashlib.sha256(change_url.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
  1639	    else:
  1640	        material = json.dumps(
  1641	            {"target_status": "under-review", "patchset": None},
  1642	            sort_keys=True,
  1643	            separators=(",", ":"),
  1644	        )
  1645	        args_hash = hashlib.sha256(material.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
  1646	    return f"{key}:{operation_type}:{args_hash}"

  1826	def fetch_ticket_labels(client: "DispatchClient", key: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
  1827	    """Return the *current* JIRA label set for ``key``.
  1828	
  1829	    OP-958 ``LabelsPropagationDrift``: the runner's ``TicketSnapshot``
  1830	    carries a point-in-time label copy taken at *selection* time (JQL
  1831	    ``fetch_pickable_tickets`` → :func:`to_snapshot`, or a single
  1832	    ``GET /issue`` for the target-override path). Operators routinely add
  1833	    ``runner:no-commits-expected`` *after* a ticket is already In
  1834	    Progress — the OP-925 R3 cascade on 2026-05-12 is the canonical
  1835	    case — so a stale snapshot makes :func:`has_ops_only_label` miss the
  1836	    sigil even though it is on the ticket. Re-reading at the no-commits
  1837	    decision point closes that drift window. Callers degrade to the
  1838	    snapshot copy if this raises (JIRA transient fault).
  1839	    """
  1840	    issue = _request(client, "GET", f"/issue/{key}?fields=labels")
  1841	    return tuple((issue.get("fields") or {}).get("labels") or ())

  1933	
  1934	def transition_back_to_todo(
  1935	    client: DispatchClient,
  1936	    key: str,
  1937	    reason: str,
  1938	    idem_key: str | None = None,
  1939	    *,
  1940	    failure_class: "str | None" = None,
  1941	    raw_traceback: str = "",
  1942	    mutex_label: str | None = None,
  1943	    area: str | None = None,
  1944	) -> None:
  1945	    """In Progress → TODO with reason comment + clear assignee.
  1946	
  1947	    OP-1541 invariant: this is a destructive recovery — it clears the
  1948	    assignee. Under a shared JIRA account ONLY the current winning
  1949	    fencing-token claim holder may revert. Callers on the TOCTOU /
  1950	    assignee-divergence path MUST first prove claim ownership (see
  1951	    :func:`is_winning_claim_owner` and ``auto-runner-jira._handle_toctou_abort``)
  1952	    so a sibling instance's assignee clear does not cascade into a false
  1953	    revert (OP-1533 burst → stoploss). The non-recovery callers (CLI
  1954	    failure, capability gate, discovered dependency, pre-claim cwd-unsafe)
  1955	    are unaffected — they own or predate the claim.
  1956	
  1957	    OP-854 (C2): when ``failure_class`` is supplied, an incident row
  1958	    lands in ``runner_incidents`` before the transition fires so the
  1959	    C8 failure-graph and the C2 recall path can pick it up next time
  1960	    the ticket is attempted. Recording is best-effort — a recorder
  1961	    fault never blocks the JIRA transition (the operator still needs
  1962	    the ticket reverted even if the audit shim is wedged).
  1963	    """
  1964	    base_key = idem_key or f"transition-{key}-back-to-todo-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
  1965	    if failure_class is not None:
  1966	        try:
  1967	            # Local import keeps jira_dispatch importable without the
  1968	            # incident_recorder + failure_class chain (e.g. early-boot
  1969	            # JQL-only smoke tests).
  1970	            from backend.agents.incident_recorder import record_runner_incident
  1971	
  1972	            record_runner_incident(
  1973	                ticket_key=key,
  1974	                failure_class=failure_class,
  1975	                summary=reason.splitlines()[0] if reason else "",
  1976	                raw_traceback=raw_traceback,
  1977	                runner_class=getattr(client, "agent_class", "unknown"),
  1978	                mutex_label=mutex_label,
  1979	                area=area,
  1980	            )
  1981	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — best-effort wiring
  1982	            log.warning(
  1983	                "jira_dispatch.record_runner_incident_failed key=%s err=%s",
  1984	                key,
  1985	                exc,
  1986	            )
  1987	    add_comment(
  1988	        client, key, f"Reverting to TODO. Reason:\n{reason}",
  1989	        idem_key=f"{base_key}-comment",
  1990	    )
  1991	    clear_assignee(client, key, idem_key=f"{base_key}-clear-assignee")
  1992	    _request_idempotent(
  1993	        client, "POST", f"/issue/{key}/transitions",
  1994	        {"transition": {"id": TRANSITION_IDS["back_to_todo"]}},
  1995	        f"{base_key}-transition",
  1996	    )
  1997	
  1998	    # OP-1140: stoploss circuit-breaker recording — best-effort.
  1999	    # A recorder fault (network blip, label-fetch 404 on a freshly-created
  2000	    # ticket, ...) must NEVER prevent the §11-revert from completing; the
  2001	    # operator still needs the ticket re-armed for triage even if the
  2002	    # counter slips one tick.
  2003	    try:
  2004	        from backend.agents import runner_stoploss
  2005	
  2006	        labels_now = fetch_labels(client, key)
  2007	        runner_stoploss.register_revert(client, key, labels_now)
  2008	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — best-effort wiring
  2009	        log.warning(
  2010	            "jira_dispatch.register_revert_failed key=%s err=%s", key, exc,
  2011	        )
  2012

  2016	    _request_idempotent(

   631	    def _jira(self) -> Any:
   632	        if self._client is None and not self._client_tried:
   633	            self._client_tried = True
   634	            try:
   635	                from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   636	
   637	                self._client = jira_dispatch.make_client(self._agent_class)
   638	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — degrade, never wedge boot
   639	                logger.warning(
   640	                    "[pipeline_coordinator] cold-start JIRA client unavailable "
   641	                    "(reconcile/sweep degraded): %s",
   642	                    exc,
   643	                )
   644	                self._client = None
   645	        return self._client

   651	        )

   654	        try:
   655	            proc = subprocess.run(
   656	                ["systemctl", "--user", "start", unit],
   657	                capture_output=True,
   658	                text=True,
   659	                timeout=self._cli_timeout,
   660	            )
   661	        except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
   662	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] systemctl start %s failed: %s", unit, exc)
   663	            return False
   664	        if proc.returncode != 0:
   665	            logger.warning(
   666	                "[pipeline_coordinator] systemctl start %s rc=%d: %s",
   667	                unit, proc.returncode, proc.stderr.strip(),
   668	            )
   669	        return proc.returncode == 0

   671	    # ── Startup-2 ──
   672	    def interrupted_tickets(self) -> list[InterruptedTicket]:
   673	        client = self._jira()
   674	        if client is None:
   675	            return []
   676	        try:
   677	            from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   678	
   679	            jql = (
   680	                f'project = "{client.project_key}" '
   681	                'AND status in ("In Progress", "進行中") '
   682	                "ORDER BY updated ASC"
   683	            )
   684	            resp = jira_dispatch._request(
   685	                client, "POST", "/search/jql",
   686	                {"jql": jql, "fields": ["status", "updated"], "maxResults": 100},
   687	            )
   688	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   689	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] interrupted-ticket query failed: %s", exc)
   690	            return []
   691	        from backend.agents.pipeline_coordinator_rules import (
   692	            DEFAULT_CLI_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   693	            STALE_MULTIPLIER,
   694	        )
   695	
   696	        stale_after = DEFAULT_CLI_TIMEOUT_SECONDS * STALE_MULTIPLIER
   697	        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   698	        out: list[InterruptedTicket] = []
   699	        for issue in resp.get("issues", []):
   700	            fields = issue.get("fields") or {}
   701	            status = str((fields.get("status") or {}).get("name", ""))
   702	            # ``updated`` is a coarse last-activity proxy for staleness (the
   703	            # exact 進行中-entry time needs the changelog; updated is a safe,
   704	            # cheaper lower bound — if it's old, the ticket is certainly stale).
   705	            stale = False
   706	            updated_raw = fields.get("updated")
   707	            if isinstance(updated_raw, str):
   708	                try:
   709	                    updated = datetime.fromisoformat(updated_raw.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
   710	                    stale = (now - updated).total_seconds() > stale_after
   711	                except ValueError:
   712	                    pass
   713	            out.append(
   714	                InterruptedTicket(key=issue.get("key", "?"), status=status, stale=stale)
   715	            )
   716	        return out
   717

  1544	    def _read_recent_decision_log(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1545	        """Return decision-log records from the last :data:`L6_REPLAY_WINDOW_HOURS`.
  1546	
  1547	        Reads the day-partitioned JSONL files spanning the window (today + the
  1548	        days it reaches back into), in chronological order, skipping malformed
  1549	        lines so a single torn record never aborts recovery.
  1550	        """
  1551	        now = self._clock()
  1552	        window_start = now - timedelta(hours=L6_REPLAY_WINDOW_HOURS)
  1553	        records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1554	        day = window_start.date()
  1555	        seen_paths: list[Path] = []
  1556	        while day <= now.date():
  1557	            path = self._decision_log.directory / f"{day.isoformat()}.jsonl"
  1558	            if path.exists():
  1559	                seen_paths.append(path)
  1560	            day = day + timedelta(days=1)
  1561	        for path in seen_paths:
  1562	            try:
  1563	                lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
  1564	            except OSError:
  1565	                continue
  1566	            for line in lines:
  1567	                if not line.strip():
  1568	                    continue
  1569	                try:
  1570	                    rec = json.loads(line)
  1571	                except json.JSONDecodeError:
  1572	                    continue
  1573	                if isinstance(rec, dict):
  1574	                    ts = record_ts(rec)
  1575	                    if ts is None or ts >= window_start:
  1576	                        records.append(rec)
  1577	        return records
  1578	
  1579	    def _startup_2_reconcile(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  1580	        """Startup-2: reconcile each interrupted (進行中) ticket (ADR §3.3).
  1581	
  1582	        Decision tree per ticket with no live runner:
  1583	          (b) Gerrit change exists + mergeable      → transition Under Review
  1584	          (a) feature branch has commits             → mark resumable, leave
  1585	          (c) no commits + stale (>2× CLI timeout)   → revert To Do (clear claim)
  1586	          (d) no commits + not yet stale (ambiguous) → @-operator, leave alone
  1587	        A ticket whose runner is still alive is left untouched (it's handling
  1588	        it). (b) is checked before (a): a mergeable change means the work is
  1589	        *finished* and only missed its transition, which beats re-running it.
  1590	        (c) vs (d) hinges on staleness so a runner that only just started (and
  1591	        hasn't committed yet) is never aggressively reverted out from under.
  1592	        """
  1593	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  1594	        for ticket in gw.interrupted_tickets():
  1595	            key = ticket.key
  1595	            key = ticket.key
  1596	            if gw.has_live_runner(key):
  1597	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "live-runner", "leave")
  1598	            elif gw.gerrit_change_mergeable(key):
  1599	                gw.transition_under_review(key)
  1600	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "gerrit-mergeable", "under_review")
  1601	            elif gw.branch_has_commits(key):
  1602	                gw.mark_resumable(key)
  1603	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "branch-has-commits", "resume")
  1604	            elif ticket.stale:
  1605	                # No commits, no live runner, long-stale → abandoned work.
  1606	                gw.reset_to_todo(key)
  1607	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "no-commits-stale", "revert_todo")
  1608	            else:
  1609	                # No commits but recent — a runner may have only just started.
  1610	                gw.mention_operator(
  1611	                    key,
  1612	                    "[cold-start Startup-2(d)] interrupted with no commits but "
  1613	                    "recent activity — ambiguous; leaving in place for operator review.",
  1614	                    urgency="medium",
  1615	                )
  1616	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "ambiguous", "operator")
  1617	
  1618	    def _record_reconcile(
  1619	        self, report: ColdStartReport, key: str, classification: str, action: str
  1620	    ) -> None:
  1621	        entry = {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 2, "ticket": key,
  1622	                 "classification": classification, "action": action}
  1623	        report.reconciled.append({"ticket": key, "classification": classification, "action": action})
  1624	        self._log_cold_start(entry)
  1625	
  1626	    def _startup_3_sweep(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  1627	        """Startup-3: drop orphaned claim:* / dead waiting-* + clear assignees."""
  1628	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  1629	        plan = gw.stale_sweep_plan()
  1630	        for key, labels in plan.stale_claims.items():
  1631	            for label in labels:
  1632	                gw.remove_label(key, label)
  1633	            gw.clear_assignee(key)
  1634	            self._record_sweep(report, key, "stale-claim", list(labels))
  1635	        for key in plan.orphan_assignees:
  1636	            gw.clear_assignee(key)
  1637	            self._record_sweep(report, key, "orphan-assignee", [])
  1638	        for key, labels in plan.resolved_waiting.items():
  1639	            for label in labels:
  1640	                gw.remove_label(key, label)
  1641	            self._record_sweep(report, key, "resolved-waiting", list(labels))
  1642	
  1643	    def _record_sweep(
  1644	        self, report: ColdStartReport, key: str, kind: str, labels: list[str]
  1645	    ) -> None:
  1646	        entry = {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 3, "ticket": key,
  1647	                 "sweep": kind, "labels": labels}
  1648	        report.swept.append({"ticket": key, "sweep": kind, "labels": labels})
  1649	        self._log_cold_start(entry)
  1650	
  1651	    def _startup_4_enter_loop(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  1652	        """Startup-4: log steady-state entry (the loop itself is run() → run_forever)."""
  1653	        report.entered_loop = True
  1654	        self._log_cold_start(
  1655	            {"event": STEADY_STATE_ENTERED_EVENT, "ts_entered": self._clock().isoformat()}
  1656	        )
  1657	        logger.info(
  1658	            "[pipeline_coordinator] cold-start complete — entered steady state at %s",
  1659	            self._clock().isoformat(),
  1660	        )

  1664	        *,
  1665	        install_signals: bool = True,

     1	"""SQLite-backed idempotency keys for runner external mutations.
     2	
     3	The runner retries whole ticks.  Without a local dedup store, retried
     4	JIRA comments and transitions can create duplicate operator-visible
     5	side effects.  This module keeps successful mutation responses for 24h
     6	under ``~/.config/omnisight/idem-keys.db`` and returns the cached
     7	response when the same key is seen again.
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import functools
    13	import json
    14	import os
    15	import sqlite3
    16	import time
    17	from pathlib import Path
    18	from typing import Any, Callable, TypeVar
    19	
    20	
    21	def _resolve_idempotency_db_path() -> Path:
    22	    """Resolve the SQLite path from env override or fall back to the legacy default.
    23	
    24	    OP-783: a multi-instance runner host runs several runner processes in
    25	    parallel; each one needs its own idempotency DB so that two
    26	    in-flight transitions for the same JIRA key (one per instance, both
    27	    targeting different tickets) don't share a row and silently dedup
    28	    each other. The launcher script sets ``OMNISIGHT_IDEMPOTENCY_DB_PATH``
    29	    to ``~/.config/omnisight/idem-keys-<bot>.db``; default-instance
    30	    runners and library callers without the env var keep the legacy
    31	    ``~/.config/omnisight/idem-keys.db`` path (backwards-compat).
    32	    """
    33	    override = os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_IDEMPOTENCY_DB_PATH", "").strip()
    34	    if override:
    35	        return Path(override).expanduser()
    36	    return Path("~/.config/omnisight/idem-keys.db").expanduser()
    37	
    38	
    39	IDEMPOTENCY_DB = _resolve_idempotency_db_path()
    40	TTL_SECONDS = 24 * 60 * 60
    41	
    42	F = TypeVar("F", bound=Callable[..., Any])
    43	
    44	
    45	class IdempotencyStore:
    46	    """Tiny SQLite cache keyed by caller-provided idempotency strings."""
    47	
    48	    def __init__(self, path: Path = IDEMPOTENCY_DB, ttl_seconds: int = TTL_SECONDS) -> None:
    49	        self.path = path
    50	        self.ttl_seconds = ttl_seconds
    51	
    52	    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    53	        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    54	        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.path)
    55	        conn.execute(
    56	            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS idempotency_keys ("
    57	            "key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, response TEXT NOT NULL, ts REAL NOT NULL)"
    58	        )
    59	        return conn
    60	
    61	    def prune(self, now: float | None = None) -> None:
    62	        cutoff = (now if now is not None else time.time()) - self.ttl_seconds
    63	        with self._connect() as conn:
    64	            conn.execute("DELETE FROM idempotency_keys WHERE ts < ?", (cutoff,))
    65	
    66	    def get(self, key: str, now: float | None = None) -> Any | None:
    67	        current = now if now is not None else time.time()
    68	        with self._connect() as conn:
    69	            row = conn.execute(
    70	                "SELECT response, ts FROM idempotency_keys WHERE key = ?",
    71	                (key,),
    72	            ).fetchone()
    73	            if row is None:
    74	                return None
    75	            response, ts = row
    76	            if ts < current - self.ttl_seconds:
    77	                conn.execute("DELETE FROM idempotency_keys WHERE key = ?", (key,))
    78	                return None
    79	            return json.loads(response)
    80	
    81	    def put(self, key: str, response: Any, now: float | None = None) -> None:
    82	        current = now if now is not None else time.time()
    83	        with self._connect() as conn:
    84	            conn.execute(
    85	                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO idempotency_keys (key, response, ts) "
    86	                "VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
    87	                (key, json.dumps(response, sort_keys=True, default=str), current),
    88	            )
    89	
    90	    def run(self, key: str, fn: Callable[[], Any]) -> Any:
    91	        self.prune()
    92	        cached = self.get(key)
    93	        if cached is not None:
    94	            return cached
    95	        response = fn()
    96	        self.put(key, response if response is not None else {})
    97	        return response
    98	
    99	
   100	DEFAULT_STORE = IdempotencyStore()
   101	
   102	
   103	def idempotent(key_arg: str = "idem_key", store: IdempotencyStore = DEFAULT_STORE) -> Callable[[F], F]:
   104	    """Decorator for functions that accept an ``idem_key`` keyword."""
   105	
   106	    def decorate(fn: F) -> F:
   107	        @functools.wraps(fn)
   108	        def wrapped(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
   109	            key = kwargs.get(key_arg)
   110	            if not key:
   111	                return fn(*args, **kwargs)
   112	            return store.run(str(key), lambda: fn(*args, **kwargs))
   113	
   114	        return wrapped  # type: ignore[return-value]
   115	
   116	    return decorate

See §Crash recovery. |
    29	| **N12 / Q7 dry_run** | NOT-RESOLVED / BLOCKING — nothing emits `dry_run=False` (factories default True rules.py:126; Tier-1 :904 + Tier-2 llm:1073/1132 pass through) → acting mode mutates nothing, forever | **OPEN DECISION — two options below (§dry_run), for codex round-3 to pick.** Either way v3 makes the flip explicit so acting actually mutates. |
    30	| **N13 coord-skip recheck** | PARTIAL — didn't name the label-read helper or fail-mode | **fail-CLOSED**: executor reads live labels via `jira_dispatch.get_issue_labels` (or equivalent) immediately before mutating; a read failure → `executed:False,reason:"label_read_failed"` (do NOT mutate on unknown state). See §coord-skip. |
    31	| **NEW (Sec2): destructive transition** | BLOCKER — `transition_back_to_todo` clears assignee (jira_dispatch:1991); rule context may be stale at execution time (:696) | **fresh recheck before destructive ops**: for `transition → To Do`, re-verify no-live-runner + status still 進行中 at execution time; abstain (`executed:False,reason:"context_stale"`) if changed. |
    32	| **NEW (Sec2): env wiring reality** | BLOCKER — unit uses inline `Environment=` (pipeline-coordinator.service:29-32), **no EnvironmentFile**; runbook:53 says so + use a drop-in | **activation rewritten** to a systemd drop-in (`systemctl --user edit`), NOT an `env.conf` file. See §Activation. |
    33	| **NEW (Sec3 Q11): crash window** | BLOCKER — `run_once` executes actions (:1703) BEFORE appending the tick record (:1708); death between = invisible to L6 | **intent-log before execute**: write a `tick_intent` record (planned actions + idem keys) BEFORE executing, then the outcome record after. L6 replays intent records whose outcome is missing, idempotently. See §Crash recovery. |
    34	| **NEW (Sec3): capability scope** | clarification | documented: the coordinator daemon is **not** capability-matrix gated (that vocabulary, capability_matrix.yaml:27, governs runner pickup). The flip is a **daemon env authorization**, not a runner capability. |
    35	| **NEW (Sec2/3): test updates** | — | explicit test-update list (§Tests): `test_l6_resolves_interrupted_actions_by_kind` (coldstart:473), `test_default_cold_start_gateway_shadow_vs_acting` (coldstart:954), `test_pipeline_coordinator_systemd.py:68`. |
    36	| **NEW (Sec3 NICE): file_ticket accounting** | clarification | `file_ticket` records BOTH `executed:False (file_ticket, requires_operator)` AND the `mention_operator` side effect as a distinct result line, so decision-log readers don't misread `executed:False` as "nothing happened". |
    37	
    38	---
    39	
    40	## §dry_run — the one OPEN DECISION for codex round-3 (Q7)
    41	
    42	Confirmed: no engine path emits `dry_run=False` today (rules.py:126 default,
    43	Tier-1 pass-through :904, Tier-2 pass-through llm:1073/1132). So a literal
    44	"only mutate when `dry_run=False`" executor would observe-only forever. v3 must
    45	choose where the flip happens. **Two options, trade-offs stated; codex round-3
    46	picks one** (this is the architecture fork):
    47	
    48	### Option A — executor ignores `dry_run` in acting mode
    49	`LiveActionExecutor` is constructed only on the `acting=True` path
    50	(build_default_coordinator:1883), so its mere existence means "mutate". It
    51	ignores the per-action `dry_run` flag entirely; the flag stays meaningful only
    52	for `ShadowActionExecutor`.
    53	- **+** smallest diff (no engine/factory change); single source of truth = the
    54	  env-gate + which executor is wired.
    55	- **+** no risk of a half-flipped state (some actions real, some not).
    56	- **−** the per-action `dry_run` field becomes dead in acting mode; a future
    57	  per-action dry-run (e.g. "shadow just this risky kind") needs new plumbing.
    58	- **−** decision-log `dry_run:true` lines (logged from the action, :1358) would
    59	  be misleading in acting mode unless the executor overwrites the logged flag.
    60	
    61	### Option B — engine seam rewrites actions to `dry_run=False` when acting
    62	A thin wrapper between `engine.evaluate` and the executor (run_once:1699-1703)
    63	rebuilds each `Action` with `dry_run=False` when `acting=True`.
    64	- **+** `dry_run` stays a real per-action property end-to-end; decision-log is
    65	  accurate; enables future per-kind shadow-in-acting.
    66	- **+** executor logic is uniform (always "mutate iff `dry_run=False`").
    67	- **−** larger diff; `Action` is a frozen dataclass (rules.py:80) → rebuild via
    68	  `dataclasses.replace`, touching every action each tick.
    69	- **−** a second place (the wrapper) must stay in sync with the env-gate.
    70	
    71	**v3 recommendation: Option A** (smallest blast radius for a first live flip;
    72	the per-action dry-run nicety is YAGNI today). But this is the fork we want
    73	codex to rule on with fresh eyes.
    74	
    75	---
    76	
    77	## Idempotency (B3 / Q8) — corrected grain
    78	
    79	- Key = `coord:{kind}:{target}:{content_hash}` where `content_hash` is a stable
    80	  digest of the action's semantic payload (`params`), **independent of
    81	  `decision_id`** — so the same logical action on two ticks dedupes (the bug
    82	  round-2 caught: decision_id is fresh per tick, rules.py:266).
    83	- Composite actions issue multiple REST calls; `_request_idempotent` caches by
    84	  key (jira_dispatch.py:331), so each call gets a distinct **suffix**:
    85	  `:add:<label>`, `:remove:<label>`, `:clear-assignee`, `:transition`,
    86	  `:comment`. (Otherwise a relabel adding 2 labels would dedupe the 2nd PUT.)
    87	- The named low-level helpers already accept `idem_key`
    88	  (`transition_to_under_review_if_needed` :1649, `transition_back_to_todo`
    89	  :1934, comment/label/assignee :2014) — **no signature change needed**, only
    90	  key discipline.
    91	
    92	---
    93	
    94	## §LiveActionExecutor dispatch (carried from v2, unchanged vocabulary)
    95	
    96	Dispatch on `action.kind` (sprint_replan.py:141 pattern) → low-level
    97	`jira_dispatch` via a client built from `config.jira_agent_class` (fixes S9):
    98	
    99	| kind | live op | idem suffix |
   100	|---|---|---|
   101	| `transition` | `transition_back_to_todo` / `transition_to_under_review_if_needed` per `to_status` + **destructive-recheck for To Do** | `:transition` |
   102	| `relabel` | `add_label`/`remove_label`; ride-along `clear_assignee` on claim-label removal | `:add:<l>`/`:remove:<l>`/`:clear-assignee` |
   103	| `mention_operator` | `add_comment(@op)`; label iff `urgency=="high"` (:807) | `:comment`(+`:label`) |
   104	| `escalate` | `add_label("needs-operator-action")` **+** `add_comment(reason)` — always both (B2) | `:label`+`:comment` |
   105	| `mark_for_followup` | `add_label("coord-resume-after:{when}")` + comment | `:label`+`:comment` |
   106	| `file_ticket` | NOT auto-executed → `mention_operator` + 2 result lines (accounting fix) | `:comment`+`:label` |
   107	| `noop` / unknown | none → `executed:False` (`reason:"unsupported"` for unknown) | — |
   108	
   109	Outcome: real `executed:True/False` because we call low-level helpers (which
   110	raise on failure) inside a per-action `try/except`, NOT the fail-open gateway
   111	that swallows exceptions (:746/:762/:810).
   112	
   113	---
   114	
   115	## §Blast-radius (B4) — cap + fairness
   116	
   117	`run_once` (:1699-1707) gains:
   118	1. `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_MAX_ACTIONS_PER_TICK` (default **1**). Excess →
   119	   `executed:False,reason:"tick_cap"`, logged.
   120	2. **Fairness cursor**: a persisted round-robin over targets so consecutive
   121	   capped ticks drain a backlog rather than re-picking the same focal ticket
   122	   (addresses round-2's starvation concern for one-action-per-ticket sweeps).
   123	3. Per-action `try/except` so one failure logs + the tick still records (S7).
   124	4. Kill-switch `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_ACTING_KILL=1` → observe-only without
   125	   restart.
   126	
   127	---
   128	
   129	## §Cold-start budget (B6) — per-phase accounting
   130	
   131	Distinct caps, each defining "mutation" for its phase:
   132	- Startup-1: `start_unit` systemctl starts (:653) — cap N₁.
   133	- Startup-2: JIRA transitions/labels/comments (:735) — cap N₂.
   134	- Startup-3: stale-sweep relabel/clear loop (:776) — cap N₃.
   135	Env: `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_COLD_START_MAX_{INFRA,RECONCILE,SWEEP}`. First flip
   136	in an env with a known-small interrupted set; `cold_start_complete=[]` is NOT a
   137	safety property (round-1 B6).
   138	
   139	---
   140	
   141	## §Crash recovery (S10 / Q11) — intent-log + persisted outcomes
   142	
   143	1. **Intent-before-execute**: `run_once` writes a `tick_intent` record (planned
   144	   actions + their idem keys) BEFORE executing (closes the :1703-before-:1708
   145	   window). After execution it writes the outcome record.
   146	2. **Persist `action_results`** alongside `actions` in the tick record (:1394)
   147	   so L6 reads per-subcall outcomes.
   148	3. L6 (`_resolve_interrupted_actions` :1514) replays any `tick_intent` whose
   149	   outcome record is missing, re-issuing under the SAME stable idem key →
   150	   idempotent, no double-apply. Recovery stays keyed on the ADR `ACTION_*`
   151	   vocabulary (no gateway-method names introduced).
   152	
   153	---
   154	
   155	## §coord-skip / quarantine recheck (N13) — fail-closed
   156	Before any mutation the executor re-reads live labels of `action.target`
   157	(`jira_dispatch.get_issue_labels` or equivalent) and:
   158	- `coord-skip`/`coord-quarantine` present → `executed:False,reason:"coord_skip"`.
   159	- label read FAILS → `executed:False,reason:"label_read_failed"` (fail-CLOSED;
   160	  never mutate on unknown state). Closes the Tier-2 gap (rules.py:611 guards
   161	  only Tier-1).
   162	
   163	---
   164	
   165	## §Activation (rewritten — repo reality)
   166	The deployed unit sets env **inline** via `Environment=`
   167	(pipeline-coordinator.service:29-32); there is **no EnvironmentFile**
   168	(coordinator-runbook.md:53). So activation is a **systemd drop-in**, not an
   169	`env.conf`:
   170	1. Phased: first flip in an env with a small interrupted set; verify per-phase
   171	   cold-start caps not exceeded.
   172	2. `systemctl --user edit pipeline-coordinator` → add
   173	   `Environment=OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_ACTING=1` and remove the
   174	   `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_DAILY_BUDGET_USD=0` override (both together — D5).
   175	   Confirm `MAX_ACTIONS_PER_TICK=1`.
   176	3. `systemctl --user daemon-reload && systemctl --user restart pipeline-coordinator`.
   177	4. Watch decision-log for `executed:True`; verify intent matches. Rollback =
   178	   `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_ACTING_KILL=1` (no restart) OR revert the drop-in +
   179	   restart.
   180	
   181	**Authorization scope**: this is a daemon env authorization. The coordinator
   182	daemon is NOT capability-matrix gated (capability_matrix.yaml:27 `jira_update`
   183	governs runner pickup, a different actor).
   184	
   185	---
   186	
   187	## §Tests (v3)
   188	Carried from v2 plus:
   189	- Idempotency: same logical action on two ticks (different decision_id) → ONE
   190	  effective mutation (the Q8 regression test).
   191	- Composite suffix: relabel adding 2 labels → 2 distinct PUTs, neither deduped.
   192	- Destructive recheck: `transition→To Do` with a now-live runner → abstains.
   193	- Crash window: intent written before execute; missing-outcome intent replays
   194	  idempotently.
   195	- coord-skip fail-closed: label read raises → no mutation.
   196	- **Update existing**: `test_l6_resolves_interrupted_actions_by_kind`
   197	  (coldstart:473), `test_default_cold_start_gateway_shadow_vs_acting`
   198	  (coldstart:954, assert agent_class), `test_pipeline_coordinator_systemd.py:68`
   199	  (new env assertions).
   200	
   201	---
   202	
   203	## Open questions for codex (round-3)
   204	- **Q7 (the fork)**: pick Option A (executor ignores dry_run in acting) vs
   205	  Option B (engine seam rewrites to dry_run=False). v3 recommends A.
   206	- Q12: is `content_hash` over `params` enough for the idem grain, or must it
   207	  include `kind`+`target` only (params may carry volatile fields)?
   208	- Q13: fairness cursor persistence — reuse the decision-log, or a separate
   209	  small state file? (Daemon is stateless-across-restarts by design, ADR §3.2.)
   210	- Q14: per-phase cold-start caps — safe default integers (N₁/N₂/N₃)?
   211	- Q15: anything v3 STILL misses.
   212

   214	1. Re-confirm B3/B4/B6/S10/N12/N13 + the 4 new BLOCKERs (destructive transition,
   215	   env reality, crash window Q11, dispatch) are RESOLVED, with file:line.
   216	2. Rule on the Q7 fork (A vs B) with reasoning.
   217	3. Final verdict: file-ready / file-ready-with-conditions / needs-v4.

   114	
   115	### 3.2 Process model
   116	
   117	Single long-running Python daemon. Subscribes to events from multiple sources;
   118	maintains in-memory work-graph cache (refreshed every tick); writes decisions to
   119	append-only log. Stateless across restarts: every tick re-builds the world from
   120	JIRA + bridge logs + decision log replay.
   121	
   122	```
   123	                    ┌──────────────────────┐
   124	                    │  pipeline_coordinator │
   125	                    │       (daemon)        │
   455	every 30s. If absent for > 90s:
   456	1. systemctl restart pipeline-coordinator.service
   457	2. emit watchdog-restart event
   458	3. If restart fails or restarted-then-died-again within 5min, escalate operator
   459	
   460	### L3 — Suicide check
   461	
   462	Coordinator's own loop detection: same `(ticket, action_type)` decision triggered
   463	3 times within 1h with no progress → **pause self** (set internal `paused=True`)
   464	+ emit `coordinator-self-paused` operator alert. Operator must `SIGUSR2`
   465	unpause OR fix the underlying issue.
   466	
   467	### L4 — Decision log
   468	
   469	Every decision (action + reasoning + outcome later) is one JSONL line in
   470	`~/.config/omnisight/coordinator/decision-log/YYYY-MM-DD.jsonl`. Append-only.
   471	Survives crashes. Operator-grep-able. Drives both recovery (L5) and learning (§10).
   472	
   473	### L5 — Drain on shutdown
   474	
   475	SIGTERM handler:

   461	def coordinator_jql(project_key: str) -> str:
   462	    """ADR-0021 §4 JQL for the 60s coordinator poll."""
   463	
   464	    return (
   465	        f'project = "{project_key}" '
   466	        'AND (labels is EMPTY OR labels not in ("coord-skip")) '
   467	        "AND ("
   468	        'labels = "needs-coordinator" '
   469	        'OR (status in ("Done", "Closed", "Won\'t Do", "却下") '
   470	        "AND statusCategoryChangedDate >= -1d) "

   472	        ") "

   477	
   478	# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   479	# Cold-start 4-phase recovery (29f-7 / OP-1005) — ADR-0021 §3.3 + §9 L6
   480	# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   481	#
   482	# "The coordinator is *the* canonical startup orchestrator — if it is healthy,
   483	# everything else is" (ADR §2.4). On boot it runs four phases before entering
   484	# the steady-state loop:
   485	#
   486	#   Startup-1  infra verify   — deployment-audit.sh + systemctl --user start
   487	#   (L6)       crash recovery — replay last 24h decision log (ADR §9 L6)
   488	#   Startup-2  JIRA reconcile — interrupted-ticket decision tree (§3.3)
   489	#   Startup-3  stale sweep    — orphaned claim:* / dead waiting-* markers
   490	#   Startup-4  enter loop     — log steady-state entry, hand off to run_forever
   491	#
   492	# Everything that touches infra / JIRA / git is funnelled through a single
   493	# injected :class:`ColdStartGateway` seam so the orchestration stays a pure,
   494	# deterministically-testable decision tree; the production default
   495	# (:class:`JiraDispatchColdStartGateway`) wires the real adapters and is
   496	# fail-safe (a degraded collaborator never raises out of startup — it logs +
   497	# yields an empty result so the daemon still reaches its loop).
   498	
   499	# Decision-log event names for the cold-start phases (grep-able per §9 L4).
   500	COLD_START_BEGAN_EVENT = "cold_start_began"
   501	COLD_START_COMPLETE_EVENT = "cold_start_complete"
   502	STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT = "startup_phase"
   503	CRASH_RECOVERY_EVENT = "crash_recovery_applied"
   504	STEADY_STATE_ENTERED_EVENT = "coordinator_entered_steady_state"
   505	
   506	# Label the runner reads to continue from an existing feature branch (§3.3 (a)).
   507	RESUME_FROM_FEATURE_LABEL = "runner:resume-from-feature-branch"
   508	
   509	# L6 replays at most this far back (ADR §9 L6: "last 24h of decision log").
   510	L6_REPLAY_WINDOW_HOURS = 24.0
   511	
   512	
   513	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   514	class InfraUnit:
   515	    """One expected-live unit as reported by the deployment audit (Startup-1)."""
   516	
   517	    name: str
   518	    live: bool
   519	
   520	
  1800	                if self._stop.is_set():
  1801	                    break
  1802	                # Interruptible: returns True if stop fired while waiting.
  1803	                if self._sleep(self._config.tick_interval_seconds):
  1804	                    break
  1805	        finally:
  1806	            self._drain()
  1807	        return ticks
  1808	
  1809	    # ── L5 drain on shutdown (ADR-0021 §9 L5) ──
  1810	
  1811	    def _drain(self) -> None:
  1812	        """Write the shutdown markers and exit cleanly.
  1813	
  1814	        The skeleton has no in-flight LLM consultation to finish (§9 L5
  1815	        step 1 is a no-op here); it writes the ``shutdown_began`` /
  1816	        ``shutdown_complete`` pair (step 2) so L6 crash-recovery can tell a
  1817	        clean shutdown from a crash.
  1818	        """
  1819	        drain_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
  1820	        self._decision_log.append(
  1821	            {
  1822	                "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  1823	                "event": "shutdown_began",
  1824	                "engine_version": self._engine.engine_version,
  1825	                "decision_id": drain_id,
  1826	                "pid": os.getpid(),
  1827	            }
  1828	        )
  1829	        # (29f: finish in-flight LLM consultation here, max-wait 60s.)
  1830	        self._decision_log.append(
  1831	            {
  1832	                "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  1833	                "event": "shutdown_complete",
  1834	                "engine_version": self._engine.engine_version,
  1835	                "decision_id": drain_id,
  1836	                "pid": os.getpid(),
  1837	            }
  1838	        )
  1839	        logger.info("[pipeline_coordinator] drain complete — exiting cleanly")
  1840	
  1841	    def _install_signal_handlers(self) -> None:
  1842	        if self._signals_installed:
  1843	            return
  1844	
  1845	        def _handler(signum: int, _frame: Any) -> None:
  1846	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] caught signal %d — draining", signum)
  1847	            self.request_stop()
  1848	
  1849	        for sig in (signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT):
  1850	            try:
  1851	                signal.signal(sig, _handler)
  1852	            except (ValueError, OSError) as exc:
  1853	                # Non-main thread / unsupported platform — caller drives
  1854	                # stop via request_stop() instead.
  1855	                logger.debug(
  1856	                    "[pipeline_coordinator] could not install handler for %s: %s",
  1857	                    sig, exc,
  1858	                )
  1859	        self._signals_installed = True
  1860	
  1861	
  1862	def build_default_coordinator(
  1863	    config: CoordinatorConfig | None = None,
  1864	    *,
  1865	    acting: bool = False,
  1866	    action_executor: ActionExecutor | None = None,
  1867	) -> PipelineCoordinator:
  1868	    """Production wiring: env config, hybrid Tier-1+Tier-2 engine, shadow layer.
  1869	
  1870	    29f-3 shipped the Tier-1 rule engine; 29f-6 fronts it with the Tier-2 LLM
  1871	    consultant (:class:`HybridDecisionEngine`) so a tick that no Tier-1 rule
  1872	    matches (or that the personality mode escalates) consults the claude CLI
  1873	    under a daily budget cap. Constructing the engine logs the loaded rule
  1874	    registry (Deploy AC). The budget guard rebuilds the day's spend from the
  1875	    decision log (ADR §3.2 stateless-across-restarts). The action layer
  1876	    defaults to shadow (observe-only) so this stays safe for the 29f-14 7-day
  1877	    shadow canary. ``acting=True`` is the single switch that permits the main
  1878	    action executor, the hourly sprint re-plan handler, AND the 4-phase
  1879	    cold-start recovery to mutate; ``acting=False`` wires every one of them
  1880	    observe-only (cold-start via :class:`ShadowColdStartGateway`, OP-1555).
  1881	    """
  1882	    config = config or CoordinatorConfig.from_env()
  1883	    if acting and action_executor is None:
  1884	        raise ValueError("acting=True requires an explicit action_executor")
  1885	    if not acting and action_executor is not None:
  1886	        raise ValueError("action_executor requires acting=True")
  1887	    decision_log = DecisionLog(config.decision_log_dir)
  1888	    # 29f-11: the learning loop reads + appends to the same decision log; build
  1889	    # it from the log so its daily/weekly schedule survives a restart (§3.2).
  1890	    from backend.agents.learning_loop import build_default_learning_loop
  1891	
  1892	    return PipelineCoordinator(
  1893	        config,
  1894	        engine=build_hybrid_engine(decision_log_dir=config.decision_log_dir),
  1895	        action_executor=action_executor,
  1896	        cold_start_gateway=_default_cold_start_gateway(config, acting=acting),
  1897	        sprint_replan_handler=build_default_sprint_replan_handler(
  1898	            config_dir=config.config_dir,
  1899	            decision_log_dir=config.decision_log_dir,
  1900	            capacity_path=config.capacity_path,
  1901	            agent_class=config.jira_agent_class,
  1902	            shadow=not acting,
  1903	        ),
  1904	        decision_log=decision_log,
  1905	        learning_loop=build_default_learning_loop(decision_log),
  1906	    )
  1907	
  1908	
  1909	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
  1910	    """CLI entrypoint. ``--once`` runs a single tick; default runs forever."""
  1911	    import argparse
  1912	
  1913	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  1914	    parser.add_argument(
  1915	        "--once",
  1916	        action="store_true",
  1917	        help="run a single tick (heartbeat + one no-op decision) and exit "
  1918	             "(skips cold-start recovery — a diagnostic tick, not a boot)",
  1919	    )
  1920	    parser.add_argument(
  1921	        "--no-cold-start",
  1922	        action="store_true",
  1923	        help="enter the steady-state loop directly, skipping the 4-phase "
  1924	             "cold-start recovery (ADR §3.3) — for local debugging only",
  1925	    )
  1926	    parser.add_argument(
  1927	        "--config-dir",
  1928	        type=Path,
  1929	        default=None,
  1930	        help="override the coordinator config dir (default ~/.config/omnisight/coordinator)",
  1931	    )
  1932	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
  1933	
  1934	    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
  1935	    config = CoordinatorConfig.from_env(config_dir=args.config_dir)
  1936	    coordinator = build_default_coordinator(config)
  1937	    if args.once:
  1938	        coordinator.run_once()
  1939	        return 0
  1940	    if args.no_cold_start:
  1941	        coordinator.run_forever()
  1942	        return 0
  1943	    # Production boot: 4-phase cold-start recovery, then steady-state loop.
  1944	    coordinator.run()
  1945	    return 0
  1946	
  1947	
  1948	if __name__ == "__main__":
  1949	    import sys
  1950	
  1951	    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

   709	    """§5.1 #2 — ``claim:default:*`` > 2× CLI timeout, no live runner: clear.
   710	
   711	    Removes the orphaned claim label(s) (ADR §3.3 Startup-3 stale-state sweep

   263	# ── Action layer (ADR-0021 §6) — shadow stub for 29f-3 ────────────────
   264	
   265	
   266	class ShadowActionExecutor:
   267	    """Records actions instead of executing them (ADR §6 / shadow mode).
   268	
   269	    The real §6 action layer (JIRA mutate / @-operator) lands in a later
   270	    phase; until then — and during the 29f-14 7-day shadow canary — the
   271	    coordinator must *observe* what a rule would do without touching JIRA.
   272	    This executor returns a per-action result record (``executed=False``)
   273	    that the daemon folds into the decision-log line, so the integration AC
   274	    ("rule returns Action → action layer executes it (mock if not yet built)
   275	    → action recorded in decision log") is satisfied without any mutation.
   276	
   277	    A real executor is a drop-in: same ``execute`` signature, returning
   278	    ``executed=True`` plus the mutation outcome.
   279	    """
   280	
   281	    #: Stamped into each result so a decision-log reader can tell shadow
   282	    #: (observed-only) executions from real ones.
   283	    executed: bool = False
   284	
   285	    def execute(self, action: Action, ctx: DecisionContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
   286	        return {
   287	            "kind": action.kind,
   288	            "target": action.target,
   289	            "executed": self.executed,
   290	            "shadow": True,
   291	        }
   292	
   293	
   294	# Action executor seam: anything with ``execute(action, ctx) -> dict``.
   295	ActionExecutor = Callable[[Action, DecisionContext], dict]

  1145	def _is_conflict(fired: Sequence[tuple[RegisteredRule, Action]]) -> bool:
  1146	    """True when ≥2 rules fire mutually-distinct (conflicting) actions.
  1147	
  1148	    Identical actions (same kind/target/params) from two rules are not a
  1149	    conflict — they agree. A :class:`NoopAction` veto is handled before this
  1150	    is called, so any Noop here is incidental and ignored.

codex
Audit only; no files modified, no tests run.

1. **Q7 Option B wiring: PARTIAL**  
   The seam is right because `run_once` evaluates before execution at `backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1699`, executes actions before building the tick record at `:1703`, and `_build_tick_record()` derives both `actions` and top-level `dry_run` from `result.actions` at `:1358-1368`. `Action` is frozen and has `dry_run=True` by default at `backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:80-100`; Tier-1 and Tier-2 pass actions through at `pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:904-912` and `pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py:1071-1082`.  
   Condition: v4’s pseudocode only rewrites a local `actions` tuple. Implementation must also rebuild the `DecisionResult` used for `_build_tick_record()` and learning-loop writeback, e.g. `result = dataclasses.replace(result, actions=actions)`. Otherwise logging remains `dry_run:true`.

2. **Idem hash: RESOLVED**  
   Stable semantic keys are required because `decision_id` is fresh per tick at `pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:266` and idempotency dedupes exact keys only at `backend/agents/idempotency.py:90-96`. Low-level JIRA calls accept `idem_key` through `_request_idempotent()` at `backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:331-338`, transition/comment helpers at `:1649`, `:1934`, `:2014`, and volatile-field exclusion plus exact comment text inclusion is correct.

3. **N13 label helper: RESOLVED**  
   `fetch_ticket_labels(client, key)` is the real helper and rereads live labels at `backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:1826-1841`. Fail-closed on exception is the right executor behavior because Tier-1 `coord-skip` only protects the context snapshot via `operator_keep_out()` at `backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:600-613`.

4. **Destructive recheck: PARTIAL**  
   `get_issue_status()` is the correct live status helper at `backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:1586-1594`, and `transition_back_to_todo()` is destructive because it clears assignee at `:1934-1995`. `_ticket_has_live_runner()` exists at `backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:986-1016`.  
   Condition: current `_ticket_has_live_runner()` treats probe errors as “not live” by design at `:987-990`. For the live executor’s destructive `To Do` transition, unknown/probe failure should abstain as `context_stale`, either by adding a strict wrapper or documenting/implementing a fail-closed variant.

5. **Cold-start defaults INFRA/RECONCILE/SWEEP=1: RESOLVED**  
   The mutation surfaces are correctly separated: Startup-1 `start_unit()` at `backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:653-669`, Startup-2 JIRA mutations at `:735-763` and loop at `:1579-1607`, Startup-3 sweep mutations at `:1626-1641`. Defaults of `1/1/1` are reasonable for first flip.

6. **Fairness cursor persistence: RESOLVED**  
   Decision-log-derived is right. ADR blesses decision-log, heartbeat, and optional `state/` only for in-flight LLM persistence at `docs/adr/ADR-0021-release-pipeline-coordinator.md:105-107`, and says the daemon is stateless across restarts, rebuilding from JIRA, bridge logs, and decision log at `:117-120`. Do not use `state/` for this cursor unless ADR changes.

7. **Crash correlation: PARTIAL**  
   `decision_id` is acceptable as the `tick_intent` ↔ `decision_tick` key because each evaluated result gets a fresh UUID at `pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:266` and current `run_once()` has one evaluation per tick at `backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1699`. Existing L6 currently scans only `decision_tick` and shutdown records at `:1491-1505`; v4 correctly requires extending it to `tick_intent`. Existing log rotation is not a blocker because `_read_recent_decision_log()` already scans all day files spanning the 24h window at `:1544-1577`.  
   Conditions: `tick_intent` must carry the final stable idem keys, not just action payloads; replay must be limited to the same 24h window as the idempotency TTL at `backend/agents/idempotency.py:40`; and the Option B rewrite must preserve the same `decision_id` in the rebuilt `DecisionResult`.

**Q16:** Use `decision_id`; no separate `intent_id` is needed unless implementation introduces multiple intent records for one evaluated decision. If that happens, add `intent_seq`, not a whole new identity.

**Q17:** Rebuild from bounded decision-log tail across all date files in the replay window, not only today’s file. A 24h tail is consistent with current L6 and idempotency TTL; add a record-count cap only as a performance guard.

**Q18:** v4 still needs the two implementation conditions above: rebuild `DecisionResult` after Option B action replacement, and make destructive live-runner recheck fail closed on unknown.

FINAL VERDICT: file-ready-with-conditions — implement `result = replace(result, actions=actions)` after the acting dry-run flip; make destructive `To Do` runner recheck fail closed on probe uncertainty; ensure `tick_intent` stores final stable idem keys and is replayed only within the 24h idem window.

1. **Q7 Option B wiring: PARTIAL**

   Condition: v4’s pseudocode only rewrites a local `actions` tuple. Implementation must also rebuild the `DecisionResult` used for `_build_tick_record()` and learning-loop writeback, e.g. `result = dataclasses.replace(result, actions=actions)`. Otherwise logging remains `dry_run:true`.
